      Error: Hydration failed because the server rendered HTML didn't match the client. As a result this tree will be regenerated on the client. This can happen if a SSR-ed Client Component used:
      
      - A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.
      - Variable input such as `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` which changes each time it's called.
      - Date formatting in a user's locale which doesn't match the server.
      - External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
      - Invalid HTML tag nesting.

FAIL packages/react-dom/src/__tests__/ReactServerRenderingHydrationUseStore-test.js
  ● ReactDOMServerHydration › should warn when the style property differs

    assertConsoleErrorDev(expected)

    Unexpected error(s) recorded.

    - Expected errors
    + Received errors

      A tree hydrated but some attributes of the server rendered HTML didn't match the client properties. This won't be patched up. This can happen if a SSR-ed Client Component used: 
      - A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.
      - Variable input such as `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` which changes each time it's called.
      - Date formatting in a user's locale which doesn't match the server.
      - External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
      - Invalid HTML tag nesting.

  ● ReactDOMServerPartialHydration › blocks the update to hydrate first if context has changed

    Hydration failed because the server rendered text didn't match the client. As a result this tree will be regenerated on the client. This can happen if a SSR-ed Client Component used:

    - A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.
    - Variable input such as `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` which changes each time it's called.
    - Date formatting in a user's locale which doesn't match the server.
    - External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
    - Invalid HTML tag nesting.

    It can also happen if the client has a browser extension installed which messes with the HTML before React loaded.

    + - A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.

FAIL packages/react-dom/src/__tests__/ReactDOMServerPartialHydrationUseStoreContext-test.internal.js
  ● ReactDOMServerPartialHydration › blocks the update to hydrate first if context has changed

    Hydration failed because the server rendered text didn't match the client. As a result this tree will be regenerated on the client. This can happen if a SSR-ed Client Component used:

    - A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.
    - Variable input such as `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` which changes each time it's called.
    - Date formatting in a user's locale which doesn't match the server.
    - External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
    - Invalid HTML tag nesting.

    It can also happen if the client has a browser extension installed which messes with the HTML before React loaded.

    https://react.dev/link/hydration-mismatch

    -             className="hello"
                >
    +             Hi
    -             Hello
